FILE: src\app\search\page.tsx
============================================================
import {
  SearchResultsClient,
} from "@/components/search/SearchResultsClient";


type SearchPageProps = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    category?: string;
    origin?: string;
    destination?: string;
    travelers?: string;
    date?: string;
    year?: string;
  }>;
};


export default async function SearchPage({
  searchParams,
}: SearchPageProps) {

  const params =
    await searchParams;

  return (
    <SearchResultsClient
      category={
        params.category ||
        "Umrah"
      }
      origin={
        params.origin ||
        "Jakarta"
      }
      destination={
        params.destination ||
        "Makkah & Madinah"
      }
      travelers={
        params.travelers ||
        "2"
      }
      date={
        params.date ||
        ""
      }
    />
  );
}

export function AffiliateClient() {
  const { language } = useMarketplacePreferences();

  const lang: Lang =
    language === "AR" ? "AR" : language === "EN" ? "EN" : "ID";

  const t = content[lang];

  const [openFaq, setOpenFaq] = useState<number | null>(0);

  const workIcons = [Link2, Share2, CircleDollarSign];

  const toolIcons = [
    Link2,
    Megaphone,
    MousePointerClick,
    PackageCheck,
    CircleDollarSign,
    WalletCards,
    BarChart3,
    Users,
  ];

  return (
    <main className="affiliate-page affiliate-v2">
      <section className="aff2-hero">
        <div className="aff2-hero-photo" />

        <div className="affiliate-shell aff2-hero-inner">
          <div className="aff2-hero-panel">
            <div className="aff2-eyebrow">
              <Sparkles size={14} />
              {t.badge}
            </div>

            <h1>
              <span>{t.heroTitle1}</span>
              <strong>{t.heroTitle2}</strong>
            </h1>

            <p>{t.heroDescription}</p>

            <div className="aff2-hero-benefits">
              {t.heroBenefits.map((item, index) => (
                <div key={item}>
                  <span className={`is-${index + 1}`}>
                    {index === 0 ? (
                      <Users size={16} />
                    ) : index === 1 ? (
                      <Sparkles size={16} />
                    ) : (
                      <Headphones size={16} />
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <strong>{item}</strong>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="aff2-section aff2-work">
        <div className="affiliate-shell">
          <div className="aff2-heading">
            <span>{t.workLabel}</span>
            <h2>{t.workTitle}</h2>
            <p>{t.workDescription}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="aff2-work-grid">
            {t.work.map((item, index) => {
              const Icon = workIcons[index];

              return (
                <article key={item.title}>
                  <div className={`aff2-work-icon is-${index + 1}`}>
                    <Icon size={27} />
                    <b>{index + 1}</b>
                  </div>

                  <h3>{item.title}</h3>
                  <p>{item.description}</p>

                  {index < 2 ? (
                    <ArrowRight className="aff2-step-arrow" size={23} />
                  ) : null}
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="aff2-section aff2-products">
        <div className="affiliate-shell">
          <div className="aff2-heading">
            <span>{t.productLabel}</span>
            <h2>{t.productTitle}</h2>
            <p>{t.productDescription}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="aff2-product-grid">
            {t.products.map((product, index) => {
              const Icon = product.icon;

              return (
                <article key={product.title}>
                  <div
                    className={`aff2-product-photo is-${index + 1}`}
                    style={{ backgroundImage: `url("${product.image}")` }}
                  >
                    <span>
                      <Icon size={20} />
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="aff2-product-body">
                    <h3>{product.title}</h3>
                    <p>{product.description}</p>
                  </div>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="aff2-section aff2-benefits">
        <div className="affiliate-shell">
          <div className="aff2-heading">
            <span>{t.whyLabel}</span>
            <h2>{t.whyTitle}</h2>
            <p>{t.whyDescription}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="aff2-benefit-grid">
            {t.why.map((item, index) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;

              return (
                <article key={item.title}>
                  <span className={`is-${index + 1}`}>
                    <Icon size={25} />
                  </span>
                  <h3>{item.title}</h3>
                  <p>{item.description}</p>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="aff2-section aff2-dashboard">
        <div className="affiliate-shell aff2-dashboard-grid">
          <div>
            <div className="aff2-heading aff2-heading-left">
              <span>{t.dashboardLabel}</span>
              <h2>{t.dashboardTitle}</h2>
              <p>{t.dashboardDescription}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="aff2-tools">
              {t.tools.map((tool, index) => {
                const Icon = toolIcons[index];

                return (
                  <div key={tool}>
                    <span>
                      <Icon size={16} />
                    </span>
                    <strong>{tool}</strong>
                    <ArrowRight size={15} />
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="aff2-dashboard-card">
            <header>
              <div>
                <span>S</span>
                <strong>segaloka</strong>
              </div>
              <em>Affiliate Dashboard</em>
            </header>

            <div className="aff2-dashboard-body">
              <aside>
                <b>Dashboard</b>
                <span>Links</span>
                <span>Products</span>
                <span>Referrals</span>
                <span>Transactions</span>
                <span>Earnings</span>
              </aside>

              <section>
                <small>Affiliate Overview</small>
                <h3>Performance</h3>

                <div className="aff2-stats">
                  <div>
                    <span>Clicks</span>
                    <strong>—</strong>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span>Orders</span>
                    <strong>—</strong>

            <h2>{t.faqTitle}</h2>

              </a>
            </div>

            <div className="aff2-faq-list">
              {t.faqs.map(([question, answer], index) => {
                const open = openFaq === index;

                return (
                  <article
                    className={open ? "is-open" : ""}
                    key={question}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setOpenFaq(open ? null : index)}
                      aria-expanded={open}
                    >
                      <span>{question}</span>
                      <ChevronDown size={16} />
                    </button>

                    {open ? <p>{answer}</p> : null}
                  </article>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Check, Mail } from "lucide-react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";

import { useMarketplacePreferences } from "@/components/providers/MarketplacePreferencesProvider";

export default function TravelRegistrationClient() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const { language } = useMarketplacePreferences();

  const plan = searchParams.get("plan");

  const selectedPlan =
    plan === "starter"
      ? "Starter"
      : plan === "growth"
        ? "Growth"
        : null;

  const title =
    language === "EN"
      ? "Register Your Travel Business"
      : language === "AR"
        ? "سجّل شركة السفر الخاصة بك"
        : "Daftarkan Travel Anda";

  const description =
    language === "EN"
      ? "Start the Segaloka Travel onboarding process. Our team will guide the verification and activation stages."
      : language === "AR"
        ? "ابدأ عملية انضمام شركة السفر إلى Segaloka وسيساعدك فريقنا خلال مراحل التحقق والتفعيل."
        : "Mulai proses onboarding Travel Segaloka. Tim kami akan membantu proses verifikasi hingga aktivasi.";

  const formDescription =
    language === "EN"
      ? "The complete registration form will be connected to the Segaloka onboarding system in the implementation phase."
      : language === "AR"
        ? "سيتم ربط نموذج التسجيل الكامل بنظام الانضمام إلى Segaloka في مرحلة التنفيذ."
        : "Form registrasi lengkap akan dihubungkan ke sistem onboarding Segaloka pada tahap implementasi berikutnya.";

  const selectedPlanLabel =
    language === "EN"
      ? "Selected plan"
      : language === "AR"
        ? "الباقة المختارة"
        : "Paket dipilih";

  return (
    <main className="partner-feature-detail">
      <div className="partner-feature-detail-shell">
        <section className="partner-feature-hero">
          <div>
            <span className="partner-feature-eyebrow">
              SEGALOKA TRAVEL PARTNER
            </span>

            <h1>{title}</h1>

            <p className="partner-feature-lead">
              {description}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="partner-feature-content">
          {selectedPlan && (
            <div className="partner-feature-benefit">
              <span>
                <Check size={16} />
              </span>

              <p>
                {selectedPlanLabel}: {selectedPlan}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <p style={{ marginTop: 18 }}>
            {formDescription}
          </p>

          <a
            className="partner-feature-cta"
            href="mailto:partnership@segaloka.com"
          >
            <Mail
              size={16}
              style={{ marginRight: 7 }}
            />

            partnership@segaloka.com
          </a>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function getJourneyKind(value: string | null): JourneyKind {
  if (
    value === "starter" ||
    value === "growth" ||
    value === "enterprise"
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "free";
}

export default function PartnerJourneyClient() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const { language } = useMarketplacePreferences();

  const lang =
    language === "EN" || language === "AR"
      ? language
      : "ID";

  const kind = getJourneyKind(searchParams.get("type"));

  const t = copy[lang];
  const current = t[kind];

  const destination =
    kind === "starter"
      ? "/partner/travel/daftar?plan=starter"
      : kind === "growth"
        ? "/partner/travel/daftar?plan=growth"
        : kind === "enterprise"
          ? "/partner/travel/enterprise"
          : "/partner/travel/daftar";

  const Icon =
    kind === "growth"
      ? Sparkles
      : kind === "enterprise"
        ? Building2
        : Rocket;

  return (
    <main className="partner-journey-page">
      <div className="partner-journey-orb partner-journey-orb-one" />
      <div className="partner-journey-orb partner-journey-orb-two" />

        </section>

                    <p>{item.description}</p>
                  </div>

                  <ArrowRight
                    size={16}
                    className="partner-card-arrow"
                  />
                </a>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="partner-section">
        <div className="partner-shell">
          <SectionTitle
            title={t.audience.title}
            description={t.audience.description}
          />

          <div className="partner-card-grid partner-card-grid-three">
            {audienceCards.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;

              return (
                <article className="partner-audience-card" key={item.title}>
                  <div className="partner-audience-visual">
                    <Icon size={42} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="partner-audience-body">
                    <span className="partner-feature-icon">
                      <Icon size={20} />
                    </span>

                    <div>
                      <h3>{item.title}</h3>
                      <p>{item.description}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section
        className="partner-section partner-section-soft"
        id="partner-daftar"
      >
        <div className="partner-shell">
          <SectionTitle
            title={t.steps.title}
            description={t.steps.description}
          />

          <div className="partner-steps">
            <div className="partner-step-line" />

            {steps.map((step) => {
              const Icon = step.icon;

              return (
                <article className="partner-step" key={step.number}>
                  <span className="partner-step-number">
                    {step.number}
                  </span>

                  <div className="partner-step-icon">
                    <Icon size={21} />
                  </div>

                  <h3>{step.title}</h3>
                  <p>{step.description}</p>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="partner-registration-cta">
            <div>
              <span>{t.steps.ready}</span>
              <h3>{t.steps.ctaTitle}</h3>
              <p>{t.steps.ctaDescription}</p>
            </div>

            <a
              className="partner-primary-button"
              href="/partner/travel/perjalanan?type=free"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            >
              {t.steps.cta}
              <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section
        className="partner-section partner-pricing-section"
        id="partner-pricing"
      >
        <div className="partner-shell">
          <SectionTitle
            title={t.pricing.title}
            description={t.pricing.description}
          />

          <div className="partner-pricing-grid">
            {t.pricing.plans.map((plan, index) => {
              const isPopular = index === 1;
              const PlanIcon =
                index === 0 ? Rocket : index === 1 ? BarChart3 : Building2;

              return (
                <article
                  className={`partner-pricing-card ${
                    isPopular ? "is-popular" : ""
                  }`}
                  key={plan.name}
                >
                  {isPopular && (
                    <span className="partner-pricing-popular">
                      {t.pricing.popular}
                    </span>
                  )}

                  <div className="partner-pricing-heading">
                    <PlanIcon size={27} />
                    <div>
                      <h3>{plan.name}</h3>
                      <p>{plan.subtitle}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="partner-pricing-price">
                    <strong>{plan.price}</strong>
                    <span>{t.pricing.perMonth}</span>
                  </div>

                  {isPopular && (
                    <div className="partner-growth-highlights">
                      <span>
                        <Check size={13} />
                        {language === "ID"
                          ? "Website Gratis"
                          : language === "EN"
                            ? "Free Website"
                            : "موقع مجاني"}
                      </span>

                      <span>
                        <Check size={13} />
                        Omnichannel
                      </span>

                      <span>
                        <Check size={13} />
                        SegaDeals
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <ul>
                    {plan.features.map((feature) => (
                      <li key={feature}>
                        <Check size={16} />
                        <span>{feature}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>

                  <a
                    className={
                      isPopular
                        ? "partner-pricing-button primary"
                        : "partner-pricing-button"
                    }
                    href={
                      index === 0
                        ? "/partner/travel/perjalanan?type=starter"
                        : index === 1
                          ? "/partner/travel/perjalanan?type=growth"
                          : "/partner/travel/perjalanan?type=enterprise"
                    }
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  >
                    {plan.cta}
                  </a>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="partner-subscription-benefit">
            <Gem size={19} />
            <p>
              <strong>{t.pricing.benefitLabel}</strong>{" "}
              {language === "ID"
                ? "sistem pemesanan terintegrasi, pengelolaan produk lebih mudah, jangkauan pasar lebih luas, dan dukungan tim Segaloka."
                : language === "EN"
                  ? "integrated booking tools, easier product management, broader market reach, and support from the Segaloka team."
                  : "أدوات حجز متكاملة وإدارة أسهل للمنتجات ووصول أوسع إلى السوق ودعم من فريق Segaloka."}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="partner-section" id="partner-faq">
        <div className="partner-shell">
          <SectionTitle
            title={t.faq.title}
            description={t.faq.description}
          />

          <div className="partner-faq-list">
            {t.faq.items.map((faq, index) => {
              const isOpen = openFaq === index;

              return (
                <article
                  className={`partner-faq ${isOpen ? "is-open" : ""}`}
                  key={faq.question}
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-expanded={isOpen}
                    onClick={() =>
                      setOpenFaq(isOpen ? null : index)
                    }
                  >
                    <span>{faq.question}</span>
                    <ChevronDown size={17} />
                  </button>

                  {isOpen && (
                    <div className="partner-faq-answer">
                      <p>{faq.answer}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="partner-contact-section">
        <div className="partner-shell partner-contact-grid">
          <div>
            <SectionTitle
              title={t.contact.title}
              description={t.contact.description}
            />

            <a
              className="partner-contact-link"
              href="mailto:partnership@segaloka.com"
            >
              <Mail size={19} />
              partnership@segaloka.com
            </a>
          </div>

          <div className="partner-contact-visual" aria-hidden="true">
            <div className="partner-contact-circle">
              <Globe2 size={72} />
              <Plane size={39} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    
      <section
        className="partner-bottom-nav"
        aria-label={language === "ID"
          ? "Navigasi Partner Travel"
          : language === "EN"
            ? "Travel Partner navigation"
            : "التنقل لشركاء السفر"}
      >
        <div className="partner-shell">
          
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

function SectionTitle({
  title,
  description,
}: {
  title: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="partner-section-heading">
      <span className="partner-section-marker" />
      <div>
        <h2>{title}</h2>
        <p>{description}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const featured: CategoryId[] = [
  "umrah",
  "haji",
  "halal",
  "domestic",
  "international",
  "segadeals",
  "visa",
];

function getHelpImageFallbackSource(
  source: string,
): string | null {
  if (!source.endsWith(".webp")) {
    return null;
  }

  return source.slice(0, -5) + ".png";
}

function applyHelpImageFallback(
  event: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLImageElement>,
  source: string,
): void {
  const image = event.currentTarget;

  /*
   * Tahap 1:
   * canonical WebP -> PNG sementara.
   */
  if (image.dataset.helpFallback !== "png") {
    const pngFallback = getHelpImageFallbackSource(source);

    if (pngFallback) {
      image.dataset.helpFallback = "png";
      image.src = pngFallback;
      return;
    }
  }

  /*
   * Tahap 2:
   * jika PNG juga tidak tersedia,
   * gunakan visual Segaloka dan hentikan error loop.
   */
  image.onerror = null;
  image.dataset.helpFallback = "default";
  image.src = "/images/hero/hero-segaloka.png";
}
export default function HelpCenterClient() {
  const preferences = useMarketplacePreferences();

  const language = (
    preferences.language === "EN" || preferences.language === "AR"
      ? preferences.language
      : "ID"
  ) as Language;

  const t = ui[language];

  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] =
    useState<CategoryId>("umrah");
  const [activeArticleId, setActiveArticleId] =
    useState(() => {
      const firstUmrahArticle = articles.find(
        (item) => item.category === "umrah",
      );

      return firstUmrahArticle?.id ?? articles[0].id;
    });
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [submittedQuery, setSubmittedQuery] = useState("");

  const activeCategoryData =
    categories.find((item) => item.id === activeCategory) ?? categories[0];

  const activeArticle =
    articles.find((item) => item.id === activeArticleId) ??
    articles.find((item) => item.category === activeCategory) ??
    articles[0];

  const isContactArticle =
    activeArticle.id === "other-hubungi-kami";

  const categoryArticles = useMemo(
    () => articles.filter((item) => item.category === activeCategory),
    [activeCategory],
  );

  const topicText = topicUi[language];

  function syncArticleUrl(articleId: string) {
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);

    url.searchParams.set("artikel", articleId);

    const nextUrl =
      `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;

    const currentArticle =
      new URL(window.location.href)
        .searchParams
        .get("artikel");

    if (currentArticle === articleId) {
      return;
    }

    window.history.pushState(
      { articleId },
      "",
      nextUrl,
    );
  }

  function applyArticleFromUrl() {
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    const articleId = url.searchParams.get("artikel");

    if (!articleId) {
      const defaultArticle = articles.find(
        (item) => item.category === "umrah",
      );

      if (defaultArticle) {
        setActiveCategory(defaultArticle.category);
        setActiveArticleId(defaultArticle.id);
      }

      return;
    }

    const article = articles.find(
      (item) => item.id === articleId,
    );

    if (!article) return;

    setActiveCategory(article.category);
    setActiveArticleId(article.id);
    setSubmittedQuery("");
    setQuery("");
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    applyArticleFromUrl();

    function handlePopState() {
      applyArticleFromUrl();
    }

    window.addEventListener(
      "popstate",
      handlePopState,
    );

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "popstate",
        handlePopState,
      );
    };
  }, []);

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    const term = submittedQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();

    if (!term) return [];

    return articles.filter((article) => {
      const category = categories.find(
        (item) => item.id === article.category,
      );

      const searchable = [
        article.title[language],
        article.summary[language],
        ...article.content[language],
        ...(article.steps?.flatMap((step) => [
          step.title[language],
          step.description[language],
        ]) ?? []),
        category?.label[language] ?? "",
      ]
        .join(" ")
        .toLocaleLowerCase();

      return searchable.includes(term);
    });
  }, [submittedQuery, language]);

  function selectCategory(id: CategoryId) {
    setActiveCategory(id);

    const article = articles.find(
      (item) => item.category === id,
    );

    if (article) {
      setActiveArticleId(article.id);
      syncArticleUrl(article.id);
    }

    setSubmittedQuery("");
    setQuery("");
  }

  function selectArticle(article: Article) {
    setActiveCategory(article.category);
    setActiveArticleId(article.id);
    setSubmittedQuery("");
    setQuery("");

    syncArticleUrl(article.id);

    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      document
        .getElementById("help-article")
        ?.scrollIntoView({
          behavior: "smooth",
          block: "start",
        });
    });
  }

  function handleSearch(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!query.trim()) {
      setSubmittedQuery("");
      return;
    }

    setSubmittedQuery(query.trim());
  }

  return (
    <main
      className="help-page"
      dir={language === "AR" ? "rtl" : "ltr"}
    >
      <section className="help-hero">
        <div className="help-hero-overlay" />

        <div className="help-container help-hero-inner">
          <form className="help-search" onSubmit={handleSearch}>
            <Search size={22} />

            <input
              type="search"
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder={t.searchPlaceholder}
              aria-label={t.searchPlaceholder}
            />

            {query ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="help-search-clear"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery("");
                  setSubmittedQuery("");
                }}
                aria-label={t.clearSearch}
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            ) : null}
          </form>

          <nav className="help-featured-nav">
            {featured.map((id) => {
              const item = categories.find(
                (category) => category.id === id,
              )!;
              const Icon = item.Icon;

              return (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  key={id}
                  className={
                    activeCategory === id ? "is-active" : ""
                  }
                  onClick={() => selectCategory(id)}
                >
                  <span className="help-featured-icon">
                    <Icon size={20} />
                  </span>

                  <span>{item.label[language]}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="help-main">
        <div className="help-container help-layout">
          <aside className="help-sidebar">
            <h2>{t.helpCenter}</h2>

            <nav>
              {categories.map((item, index) => {
                const Icon = item.Icon;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className={
                      index === 7
                        ? "help-sidebar-group-start"
                        : undefined
                    }
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={
                        activeCategory === item.id
                          ? "is-active"
                          : ""
                      }
                      onClick={() => selectCategory(item.id)}
                    >
                      <span className="help-sidebar-icon">
                        <Icon size={20} />
                      </span>

                      <span>{item.label[language]}</span>

                      <ChevronRight
                        size={17}
                        className="help-sidebar-chevron"
                      />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </nav>
          </aside>

          <div className="help-content">
            {submittedQuery ? (
              <section className="help-search-results">
                <div className="help-breadcrumb">
                  <span>{t.breadcrumb}</span>
                  <ChevronRight size={14} />
                  <strong>{t.searchResults}</strong>
                </div>

                <div className="help-results-heading">
                  <div>
                    <span className="help-overline">
                      {t.searchResults}
                    </span>

                    <h1>
                      {t.resultFor} “{submittedQuery}”
                    </h1>

                    <p>
                      {results.length} {t.articleFound}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setQuery("");
                      setSubmittedQuery("");
                    }}
                  >
                    {t.clearSearch}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {results.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="help-result-list">
                    {results.map((article) => {
                      const category = categories.find(
                        (item) =>
                          item.id === article.category,
                      );

                      return (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          key={article.id}
                          onClick={() =>
                            selectArticle(article)
                          }
                        >
                          <span>
                            <small>
                              {category?.label[language]}
                            </small>

                            <strong>
                              {article.title[language]}
                            </strong>

                            <p>
                              {article.summary[language]}
                            </p>
                          </span>

                          <ChevronRight size={19} />
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="help-empty">
                    <Search size={30} />
                    <h2>{t.noResult}</h2>
                    <p>{t.noResultDescription}</p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </section>
            ) : (
              <article
                className="help-article"
                id="help-article"
              >
                <div className="help-breadcrumb">
                  <span>{t.breadcrumb}</span>
                  <ChevronRight size={14} />

                  <span>
                    {activeCategoryData.label[language]}
                  </span>

                  <ChevronRight size={14} />

                  <strong>
                    {activeArticle.title[language]}
                  </strong>
                </div>

                <header className="help-article-header">
                  <h1>
                    {activeArticle.title[language]}
                  </h1>
                  <p>{t.lastUpdated}</p>
                </header>

                {isContactArticle ? (
                  <section
                    className="help-contact-center"
                    aria-label={activeArticle.title[language]}
                  >
                    <p className="help-contact-intro">
                      {language === "ID"
                        ? "Pilih kanal bantuan yang paling sesuai dengan kebutuhan Anda."
                        : language === "EN"
                          ? "Choose the support channel that best fits your needs."
                          : "اختر قناة الدعم الأنسب لاحتياجاتك."}
                    </p>

                    <div className="help-contact-grid">
                      <article className="help-contact-card">
                        <div
                          className="help-contact-icon"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        >
                          <span>WA</span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="help-contact-card-copy">
                          <h2>WhatsApp</h2>

                          <p>
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Gunakan WhatsApp untuk bantuan cepat terkait layanan, pesanan, atau transaksi Segaloka."
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Use WhatsApp for quick assistance with Segaloka services, bookings, or transactions."
                                : "استخدم واتساب للحصول على مساعدة سريعة بشأن خدمات سيجالوكا أو الحجوزات أو المعاملات."}
                          </p>

                          <span className="help-contact-status">
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Segera tersedia"
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Coming soon"
                                : "قريباً"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </article>

                      <article className="help-contact-card">
                        <div
                          className="help-contact-icon"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        >
                          <span>@</span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="help-contact-card-copy">
                          <h2>Email</h2>

                          <p>
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Gunakan email untuk pertanyaan yang membutuhkan penjelasan atau dokumen pendukung."
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Use email for questions that require detailed information or supporting documents."
                                : "استخدم البريد الإلكتروني للاستفسارات التي تتطلب تفاصيل أو مستندات داعمة."}
                          </p>

                          <span className="help-contact-status">
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Segera tersedia"
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Coming soon"
                                : "قريباً"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </article>

                      <a
                        href="/pusat-bantuan"
                        className="help-contact-card help-contact-card-link"
                      >
                        <div
                          className="help-contact-icon"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        >
                          <span>?</span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="help-contact-card-copy">
                          <h2>
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Pusat Bantuan"
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Help Center"
                                : "مركز المساعدة"}
                          </h2>

                          <p>
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Cari panduan berdasarkan layanan atau topik sebelum menghubungi tim bantuan."
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Find guidance by service or topic before contacting the support team."
                                : "ابحث عن الإرشادات حسب الخدمة أو الموضوع قبل التواصل مع فريق الدعم."}
                          </p>

                          <span className="help-contact-status">
                            {language === "ID"
                              ? "Buka Pusat Bantuan →"
                              : language === "EN"
                                ? "Open Help Center →"
                                : "فتح مركز المساعدة ←"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </a>
                    </div>

                    <div className="help-contact-note">
                      <strong>
                        {language === "ID"
                          ? "Sebelum menghubungi bantuan"
                          : language === "EN"
                            ? "Before contacting support"
                            : "قبل التواصل مع الدعم"}
                      </strong>

                      <p>
                        {language === "ID"
                          ? "Untuk kendala pesanan atau transaksi, siapkan nomor pesanan dan informasi yang relevan agar proses pengecekan lebih mudah."
                          : language === "EN"
                            ? "For booking or transaction issues, prepare your booking number and relevant information to make the review process easier."
                            : "عند وجود مشكلة في حجز أو معاملة، جهّز رقم الحجز والمعلومات ذات الصلة لتسهيل عملية التحقق."}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </section>
                ) : (
                  <div className="help-article-copy">
                    {activeArticle.content[language].map(
                      (paragraph) => (
                        <p key={paragraph}>{paragraph}</p>
                      ),
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeArticle.steps?.length ? (
                  <section
                    className="help-article-steps"
                    aria-label={activeArticle.title[language]}
                  >
                    {activeArticle.steps.map((step, index) => (
                      <article
                        className="help-article-step"
                        key={`${activeArticle.id}-${index}`}
                      >
                        <div className="help-article-step-copy">
                          <span className="help-article-step-number">
                            {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                          </span>

                          <div>
                            <h2>{step.title[language]}</h2>
                            <p>{step.description[language]}</p>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {step.image ? (
                          <figure className="help-article-step-visual">
                            <img
                              src={step.image}
                              alt={step.imageAlt?.[language] ?? ""}
                              onError={(event) =>
                                applyHelpImageFallback(
                                  event,
                                  step.image!,
                                )
                              }
                              loading={index === 0 ? "eager" : "lazy"}
                            />
                          </figure>
                        ) : null}
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </section>
                ) : null}

                {categoryArticles.length > 1 ? (
                  <section className="help-category-topics">
                    <div className="help-category-topics-heading">
                      <div>
                        <span className="help-overline">
                          {topicText.articles}
                        </span>

                        <h2>{topicText.topics}</h2>

                        <p>{topicText.chooseTopic}</p>
                      </div>

                      <span className="help-topic-count">
                        {categoryArticles.length}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="help-topic-list">
                      {categoryArticles.map((article) => (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          key={article.id}
                          className={
                            article.id === activeArticle.id
                              ? "is-active"
                              : ""
                          }
                          onClick={() => selectArticle(article)}
                        >
                          <span className="help-topic-main">
                            <strong>
                              {article.title[language]}
                            </strong>

                            <p>
                              {article.summary[language]}
                            </p>
                          </span>

                          <span className="help-topic-action">
                            <span>{topicText.openArticle}</span>
                            <ChevronRight size={17} />
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </section>
                ) : null}

                {activeArticle.id ===
                "apa-itu-segaloka" ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="help-platform-visual">
                      <div className="help-platform-copy">
                        <span>
                          {t.platformEyebrow}
                        </span>

                        <strong>
                          {t.platformTitle}
                        </strong>

                        <p>
                          {t.platformDescription}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="help-device help-device-laptop">
                        <div className="help-device-screen">
                          <img
                            src="/images/hero/hero-segaloka.png"
                            alt="Segaloka"
                          />

                          <div className="help-screen-ui">
                            <span />
                            <span />
                            <span />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="help-device help-device-phone">
                        <div className="help-phone-notch" />

                        <div className="help-phone-screen">
                          <strong>Segaloka</strong>
                          <div className="help-phone-search" />

                          <div className="help-phone-icons">
                            <span />
                            <span />
                            <span />
                          </div>

                          <div className="help-phone-card" />
                          <div className="help-phone-card short" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <section className="help-services">
                      <div className="help-section-heading">
                        <h2>{t.mainServices}</h2>
                        <p>
                          {t.mainServicesDescription}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="help-service-grid">
                        {featured.map((id, index) => {
                          const item = categories.find(
                            (category) =>
                              category.id === id,
                          )!;
                          const Icon = item.Icon;

                          return (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              key={id}
                              onClick={() =>
                                selectCategory(id)
                              }
                              className={`help-service-card help-service-${index + 1}`}
                            >
                              <div className="help-service-image">
                                <img
                                  src="/images/hero/hero-segaloka.png"
                                  alt=""
                                />
                              </div>

                              <div className="help-service-body">
                                <span className="help-service-icon">
                                  <Icon size={20} />
                                </span>

                                <span className="help-service-copy">
                                  <strong>
                                    {
                                      item.label[
                                        language
                                      ]
                                    }
                                  </strong>

                                  <small>
                                    {
                                      item.description[
                                        language
                                      ]
                                    }
                                  </small>
                                </span>

                                <span className="help-service-arrow">
                                  <ChevronRight
                                    size={18}
                                  />
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </section>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <section className="help-related">
                    <div>
                      <span className="help-overline">
                        {
                          activeCategoryData.label[
                            language
                          ]
                        }
                      </span>

                      <h2>{t.needMore}</h2>
                      <p>
                        {t.needMoreDescription}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <Search size={30} />
                  </section>
                )}
              </article>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
