      "بعد اختيار عرض واحد ينتقل النظام إلى عملية المعاملة.",
    ],

    important: "الخصوصية وعملية المعاملة",
    importantItems: [
      "لا يلزم تقديم المستندات الشخصية عند إنشاء الطلب الأولي.",
      "يتم طلب بيانات المسافر الكاملة بعد اختيار العرض.",
      "يتم إنشاء صفحة أو رابط المعاملة عبر Segaloka.",
      "الدفع والمستندات المطلوبة تتبع المعاملة المختارة.",
    ],

    builderKicker: "إنشاء طلب SEGADEALS",
    builderTitle: "ما الرحلة التي تبحث عنها؟",
    builderDesc:
      "أدخل الاحتياجات الأساسية أولاً. في مرحلة الواجهة الحالية لن يتم إرسال الطلب إلى النظام الخلفي.",

    tripType: "نوع الرحلة",
    origin: "من",
    destination: "وجهة الدخول",
    travelers: "المسافرون",
    budget: "الميزانية",
    departure: "التاريخ والشهر",
    notes: "ملاحظات",
    optional: "اختياري",

    tripPlaceholder: "اختر نوع الرحلة",
    originPlaceholder: "اختر مدينة المغادرة",
    destinationPlaceholder: "اختر وجهة الدخول",
    travelerPlaceholder: "حدد عدد المسافرين",
    budgetPlaceholder: "أدخل الميزانية",
    departurePlaceholder: "اختر التاريخ والشهر",
    notesPlaceholder: "أضف متطلبات خاصة عند الحاجة",

    sendRequest: "إرسال الطلب إلى شركات السفر",
    uiNotice:
      "سيتم تفعيل إرسال الطلب بعد ربط مسار SegaDeals بالنظام الخلفي.",

    faqKicker: "الأسئلة الشائعة",
    faqTitle: "حول SegaDeals",
    faqs: [
      ["هل SegaDeals باقة سفر؟", "لا. تتيح SegaDeals للمسافر إنشاء طلب واستقبال عروض من شركات السفر المناسبة."],
      ["من يستقبل طلبي؟", "فقط شركات السفر المؤهلة لنوع الرحلة والتي فعّلت SegaDeals."],
      ["هل يجب الالتزام بميزانيتي تماماً؟", "الميزانية مرجع للاحتياج ويمكن لشركة السفر تقديم السعر والخدمات المناسبة."],
      ["هل يجب أن أختار أحد العروض؟", "لا. يمكنك مقارنة العروض واختيار الأنسب لك."],
      ["متى أقدم مستندات المسافر؟", "يتم طلب البيانات والمستندات الكاملة بعد اختيار العرض وإنشاء المعاملة."],
      ["كيف يتم الدفع؟", "بعد اختيار العرض ينشئ Segaloka مسار المعاملة للانتقال إلى الدفع."],
    ],
  },
} as const;

const benefitIcons = [
  Search,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
];

const stepIcons = [
  FileText,
  Search,
  Send,
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  WalletCards,
];

export default function SegaDealsClient() {
  const {
    language,
    direction,
    currency,
    locale,
  } = useMarketplacePreferences();

  const [tripType, setTripType] =
    useState<SegaDealsTripType>("Umrah");

  /* SEGADEALS_PICKER_V9 */
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState("");
  const [originQuery, setOriginQuery] = useState("");
  const [originOpen, setOriginOpen] = useState(false);

  const [destination, setDestination] = useState("");
  const [customDestination, setCustomDestination] = useState("");

  const [destinationOpen, setDestinationOpen] =
    useState(false);

  const [destinationQuery, setDestinationQuery] =
    useState("");

  /* SEGADEALS_PICKER_OUTSIDE_V11 */
  const [dateOpen, setDateOpen] =
    useState(false);

  const [calendarCursor, setCalendarCursor] =
    useState(() => {
      const now = new Date();

      return new Date(
        now.getFullYear(),
        now.getMonth(),
        1,
      );
    });

  const [travelers, setTravelers] =
    useState<SegaDealsTraveler>({
      adult: 1,
      child: 0,
      infant: 0,
    });

  const [budget, setBudget] = useState("");
  const [departureDate, setDepartureDate] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const [requestStage, setRequestStage] =
    useState<"form" | "review" | "confirmed">("form");

  useEffect(() => {
    const closePickersFromOutside = (
      event: PointerEvent,
    ) => {
      const target = event.target;

      if (!(target instanceof Element)) {
        return;
      }

      /*
       * Klik di dalam trigger / popover tidak boleh
       * menutup picker sebelum event internal selesai.
       */
      if (
        target.closest(
          ".segadeals-picker-anchor, .segadeals-combobox, .segadeals-picker-popover",
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      setOriginOpen(false);
      setDestinationOpen(false);
      setDateOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener(
      "pointerdown",
      closePickersFromOutside,
    );

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener(
        "pointerdown",
        closePickersFromOutside,
      );
    };
  }, []);

  const filteredOrigins = useMemo(() => {
    const query = originQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();

    if (!query) {
      return segaDealsIndonesiaCities;
    }

    return segaDealsIndonesiaCities.filter((city) =>
      city.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query),
    );
  }, [originQuery]);

  const destinationOptions =
    segaDealsDestinations[tripType];

  const selectedDestination =
    tripType === "Custom"
      ? customDestination.trim()
      : destination;

  const filteredDestinations = useMemo(() => {
    const query =
      destinationQuery
        .trim()
        .toLocaleLowerCase();

    if (!query) {
      return destinationOptions;
    }

    return destinationOptions.filter((item) =>
      item.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query),
    );
  }, [destinationOptions, destinationQuery]);

  const calendarLocale =
    language === "ID"
      ? "id-ID"
      : language === "AR"
        ? "ar-SA"
        : "en-US";

  const calendarMonthFormatter =
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat(calendarLocale, {
      month: "long",
      year: "numeric",
    });

  const calendarWeekdays =
    language === "ID"
      ? ["Min", "Sen", "Sel", "Rab", "Kam", "Jum", "Sab"]
      : language === "AR"
        ? ["أحد", "اثن", "ثلا", "أرب", "خمي", "جمع", "سبت"]
        : ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];

  const toDateKey = (date: Date) => {
    const year = date.getFullYear();

    const month = String(
      date.getMonth() + 1,
    ).padStart(2, "0");

    const day = String(
      date.getDate(),
    ).padStart(2, "0");

    return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
  };

  const buildCalendarDays = (
    monthDate: Date,
  ) => {
    const year = monthDate.getFullYear();
    const month = monthDate.getMonth();

    const firstDay =
      new Date(year, month, 1).getDay();

    const totalDays =
      new Date(
        year,
        month + 1,
        0,
      ).getDate();

    const previousMonthDays =
      new Date(
        year,
        month,
        0,
      ).getDate();

    const cells: Array<{
      date: Date;
      currentMonth: boolean;
    }> = [];

    for (
      let offset = firstDay - 1;
      offset >= 0;
      offset -= 1
    ) {
      cells.push({
        date: new Date(
          year,
          month - 1,
          previousMonthDays - offset,
        ),
        currentMonth: false,
      });
    }

    for (
      let day = 1;
      day <= totalDays;
      day += 1
    ) {
      cells.push({
        date: new Date(
          year,
          month,
          day,
        ),
        currentMonth: true,
      });
    }

    let nextDay = 1;

    while (cells.length < 42) {
      cells.push({
        date: new Date(
          year,
          month + 1,
          nextDay,
        ),
        currentMonth: false,
      });

      nextDay += 1;
    }

    return cells;
  };

  const calendarSecondMonth =
    new Date(
      calendarCursor.getFullYear(),
      calendarCursor.getMonth() + 1,
      1,
    );

  const calendarMonths = [
    calendarCursor,
    calendarSecondMonth,
  ];

  const chooseDestination = (
    value: string,
  ) => {
    setDestination(value);
    setDestinationQuery("");

    setOriginOpen(false);
    setDestinationOpen(false);
    setDateOpen(false);

    setRequestStage("form");
  };

  const chooseDepartureDate = (
    date: Date,
  ) => {
    const value = toDateKey(date);

    if (value < minimumDate) {
      return;
    }

    setDepartureDate(value);

    setOriginOpen(false);
    setDestinationOpen(false);
    setDateOpen(false);

    setRequestStage("form");
  };

  const moveCalendar = (
    delta: number,
  ) => {
    setCalendarCursor((current) =>
      new Date(
        current.getFullYear(),
        current.getMonth() + delta,
        1,
      ),
    );
  };

  const travelerTotal =
    getSegaDealsTravelerTotal(travelers);

  const numericBudget =
    Number(budget.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""));

  const isRequestValid =
    origin.trim().length > 0 &&
    selectedDestination.length > 0 &&
    travelerTotal > 0 &&
    numericBudget > 0 &&
    departureDate.length > 0;

  const minimumDate = new Date()
    .toISOString()
    .slice(0, 10);

  const budgetDisplay = numericBudget
    ? new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
        style: "currency",
        currency,
        maximumFractionDigits:
          currency === "IDR" ? 0 : 2,
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
      }).format(numericBudget)
    : "-";

  const departureDisplay = departureDate
    ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
        day: "numeric",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
      }).format(
        new Date(`${departureDate}T00:00:00`),
      )
    : "-";

  const updateTraveler = (
    key: keyof SegaDealsTraveler,
    delta: number,
  ) => {
    setTravelers((current) => {
      const next = Math.max(0, current[key] + delta);

      if (
        key === "adult" &&
        next === 0 &&
        current.child === 0 &&
        current.infant === 0
      ) {
        return current;
      }

      return {
        ...current,
        [key]: next,
      };
    });
  };

  const chooseTripType = (
    nextType: SegaDealsTripType,
  ) => {
    setTripType(nextType);
    setDestination("");
    setDestinationQuery("");
    setDestinationOpen(false);
    setCustomDestination("");
    setRequestStage("form");
  };

  const chooseOrigin = (city: string) => {
    setOrigin(city);
    setOriginQuery(city);

    setOriginOpen(false);
    setDestinationOpen(false);
    setDateOpen(false);
  };

  const resetRequest = () => {
    setTripType("Umrah");
    setOrigin("");
    setOriginQuery("");
    setOriginOpen(false);
    setDestination("");
    setDestinationQuery("");
    setDestinationOpen(false);
    setCustomDestination("");
    setDateOpen(false);
    setTravelers({
      adult: 1,
      child: 0,
      infant: 0,
    });
    setBudget("");
    setDepartureDate("");
    setNotes("");
    setRequestStage("form");
  };

  const t =
    copy[(language as Language) ?? "ID"] ??
    copy.ID;

  const scrollToRequest = () => {
    document
      .getElementById("segadeals-request")
      ?.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: "smooth",
        block: "start",
      });
  };

  const scrollToHow = () => {
    document
      .getElementById("segadeals-how")
      ?.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: "smooth",
        block: "start",
      });
  };

  return (
    <main
      className="segadeals-page"
      dir={direction}
      lang={
        language === "AR"
          ? "ar"
          : language === "EN"
            ? "en"
            : "id"
      }
    >
      <section className="segadeals-hero">
        <div className="segadeals-shell segadeals-hero-inner">
          <div className="segadeals-breadcrumb">
            <a href="/">{t.home}</a>
            <span>/</span>
            <strong>SegaDeals</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="segadeals-hero-grid">
            <div className="segadeals-hero-copy">
              <span className="segadeals-eyebrow">
                {t.heroKicker}
              </span>

              <h1>{language === "ID" ? (
  <>
    Tentukan perjalanan Anda. Biarkan Travel memberikan{" "}
    <strong>penawaran terbaik.</strong>
  </>
) : (
  t.heroTitle
)}</h1>
              <p>{t.heroDesc}</p>

              <div className="segadeals-hero-actions">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="segadeals-button segadeals-button-primary"
                  onClick={scrollToRequest}
                >
                  {t.createRequest}
                  <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="segadeals-button segadeals-button-secondary"
                  onClick={scrollToHow}
                >
                  {t.learn}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="segadeals-request-visual">
              <div className="segadeals-request-node is-main">
                <div className="segadeals-request-node-icon">
                  <Users size={22} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>01</span>
                  <strong>{t.request}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="segadeals-request-line" />

              <div className="segadeals-request-node">
                <div className="segadeals-request-node-icon">
                  <Search size={20} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>02</span>
                  <strong>{t.matching}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="segadeals-request-line" />

              <div className="segadeals-request-node">
                <div className="segadeals-request-node-icon">
                  <Sparkles size={20} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>03</span>
                  <strong>{t.offers}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="segadeals-section segadeals-about-section">
        <div className="segadeals-shell">
          <div className="segadeals-intro">
            <span className="segadeals-section-eyebrow">
              {t.aboutKicker}
            </span>
            <h2>{t.aboutTitle}</h2>
            <p>{t.aboutDesc}</p>
          </div>

          <h3 className="segadeals-benefit-title">
            {t.whyTitle}
          </h3>

          <div className="segadeals-benefit-grid">
            {t.benefits.map((item, index) => {
              const Icon = benefitIcons[index];

              return (
                <article
                  className="segadeals-benefit-card"
                  key={item[0]}
                >
                  <div className="segadeals-icon-box">
                    <Icon size={22} />
                  </div>
                  <h3>{item[0]}</h3>
                  <p>{item[1]}</p>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section
        className="segadeals-section segadeals-how-section"
        id="segadeals-how"
      >
        <div className="segadeals-shell">
          <div className="segadeals-section-heading">
            <span className="segadeals-section-eyebrow">
              {t.howKicker}
            </span>
            <h2>{t.howTitle}</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="segadeals-step-grid segadeals-step-grid-six">
            {t.steps.map((step, index) => {
              const Icon = stepIcons[index];

              return (
                <article
                  className="segadeals-step"
                  key={step[0]}
                >
                  <div className="segadeals-step-number">
                    {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                  </div>

                  <div className="segadeals-step-icon">
                    <Icon size={21} />
                  </div>

                  <h3>{step[0]}</h3>
                  <p>{step[1]}</p>
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section
        className="segadeals-section segadeals-request-section segadeals-request-v3"
        id="segadeals-request"
      >
        <div className="segadeals-shell">
          <div className="segadeals-builder-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="segadeals-section-eyebrow">
                {t.builderKicker}
              </span>
              <h2>{t.builderTitle}</h2>
              <p>
                Tentukan kebutuhan utama perjalanan Anda.
                Travel akan melengkapi detail hotel, maskapai,
                durasi, fasilitas, dan harga melalui penawaran.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="segadeals-builder-status">
              <ShieldCheck size={18} />
              <span>Data pribadi belum diperlukan</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="segadeals-builder-layout">
            <aside className="segadeals-builder-aside">
              <div className="segadeals-builder-aside-copy">
                <span>REQUEST → OFFER</span>
                <h3>
                  Satu kebutuhan,
                  beberapa pilihan Travel.
                </h3>
                <p>
                  Anda cukup menentukan kebutuhan utama.
                  Travel yang sesuai akan memberikan
                  penawaran untuk dibandingkan.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="segadeals-builder-collage">
                <div className="segadeals-builder-photo segadeals-builder-photo-main">
                  <div>
                    <strong>Umrah</strong>
                    <span>Makkah & Madinah</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="segadeals-builder-photo segadeals-builder-photo-second">
                  <div>
                    <strong>Halal Tour</strong>
                    <span>Jelajahi dunia</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="segadeals-builder-photo segadeals-builder-photo-third">
                  <div>
                    <strong>Domestik</strong>
                    <span>Indonesia</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="segadeals-builder-aside-flow">
                <div>
                  <span>01</span>
                  <strong>Buat request</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>02</span>
                  <strong>Terima penawaran</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>03</span>
                  <strong>Pilih Travel</strong>
                </div>
              </div>
            </aside>

            <div className="segadeals-builder-card">
              {requestStage === "form" && (
                <>
                  <div className="segadeals-builder-card-head">
                    <div>
                      <span>LANGKAH 1 DARI 2</span>
                      <h3>Detail Request</h3>
                    </div>

                    <div className="segadeals-currency-badge">
                      <CircleDollarSign size={16} />
                      {currency}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="segadeals-builder-block">
                    <div className="segadeals-builder-label">
                      <span>Jenis Perjalanan</span>
                      <small>Pilih satu</small>
                    </div>

                    <div className="segadeals-trip-types">
                      {segaDealsTripTypes.map((type) => (
                        <button
                          key={type}
                          type="button"
                          className={
                            tripType === type
                              ? "is-active"
                              : ""
                          }
                          onClick={() =>
                            chooseTripType(type)
                          }
                        >
                          <PlaneTakeoff size={17} />
                          <span>{type}</span>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="segadeals-builder-grid">
                    <div className="segadeals-builder-field">
                      <label htmlFor="segadeals-origin">
                        Dari
                      </label>

                      <div className="segadeals-control segadeals-combobox">
                        <MapPin size={18} />

                        <input
                          id="segadeals-origin"
                          type="text"
                          autoComplete="off"
                          value={originQuery}
                          placeholder="Cari kota keberangkatan"
                          onFocus={() => {
                            /* SEGADEALS_PICKER_EXCLUSIVE_V10 */
                            setOriginOpen(true);
                            setDestinationOpen(false);
                            setDateOpen(false);
                          }}
                          onChange={(event) => {
                            setOriginQuery(
                              event.target.value,
                            );
                            setOrigin("");
                            setOriginOpen(true);
                            setDestinationOpen(false);
                            setDateOpen(false);
                          }}
                        />

                        <ChevronDown size={16} />

                        {originOpen && (
                          <div className="segadeals-picker-popover segadeals-origin-picker">
                            <div className="segadeals-picker-head">
                              <strong>
                                Pilih Kota atau Bandara
                              </strong>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="segadeals-picker-close"
                                aria-label="Tutup"
                                onMouseDown={(event) =>
                                  event.preventDefault()
                                }
                                onClick={() =>
                                  setOriginOpen(false)
                                }
                              >
                                ×
                              </button>
                            </div>

                            <div className="segadeals-picker-search">
                              <Search size={18} />

                              <input
                                type="text"
                                autoComplete="off"
                                value={originQuery}
                                placeholder="Masukkan nama kota atau bandara..."
                                onChange={(event) => {
                                  setOriginQuery(
                                    event.target.value,
                                  );

                                  setOrigin("");
                                }}
                              />
                            </div>

                            <div className="segadeals-picker-section">
                              <span className="segadeals-picker-label">
                                Destinasi Populer
                              </span>

                              <div className="segadeals-picker-chips">
                                {filteredOrigins.length > 0 ? (
                                  filteredOrigins.map((city) => (
                                    <button
                                      key={city}
                                      type="button"
                                      className={
                                        origin === city
                                          ? "is-active"
                                          : ""
                                      }
                                      onMouseDown={(event) =>
                                        event.preventDefault()
                                      }
                                      onClick={() =>
                                        chooseOrigin(city)
                                      }
                                    >
                                      {city}
                                    </button>
                                  ))
                                ) : (
                                  <div className="segadeals-picker-empty">
                                    Kota atau bandara tidak ditemukan.
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="segadeals-builder-field">
                      <label htmlFor="segadeals-destination">
                        {tripType === "Umrah"
                          ? "Tujuan Masuk"
                          : "Tujuan"}
                      </label>

                      {tripType === "Custom" ? (
                        <div className="segadeals-control">
                          <MapPin size={18} />

                          <input
                            id="segadeals-destination"
                            type="text"
                            value={customDestination}
                            placeholder="Tulis tujuan perjalanan"
                            onChange={(event) =>
                              setCustomDestination(
                                event.target.value,
                              )
                            }
                          />
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="segadeals-picker-anchor">
                          <button
                            id="segadeals-destination"
                            type="button"
                            className={[
                              "segadeals-control",
                              "segadeals-picker-trigger",
                              destination
                                ? "has-value"
                                : "",
                              destinationOpen
                                ? "is-open"
                                : "",
                            ]
                              .filter(Boolean)
                              .join(" ")}
                            aria-haspopup="dialog"
                            aria-expanded={destinationOpen}
                            onClick={() => {
                              const nextOpen =
                                !destinationOpen;

                              setOriginOpen(false);
                              setDateOpen(false);
                              setDestinationOpen(
                                nextOpen,
                              );
                            }}
                          >
                            <MapPin size={18} />

                            <span>
                              {destination ||
                                (tripType === "Umrah"
                                  ? "Pilih tujuan Umrah"
                                  : "Pilih tujuan")}
                            </span>

                            <ChevronDown size={16} />
                          </button>

                          {destinationOpen && (
                            <div className="segadeals-picker-popover segadeals-destination-picker">
                              <div className="segadeals-picker-head">
                                <strong>
                                  Pilih Tujuan
                                </strong>

                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  className="segadeals-picker-close"
                                  aria-label="Tutup"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    setDestinationOpen(false)
                                  }
                                >
                                  ×
                                </button>
                              </div>

                              <div className="segadeals-picker-search">
                                <Search size={18} />

                                <input
                                  type="text"
                                  autoComplete="off"
                                  value={destinationQuery}
                                  placeholder={
                                    tripType === "Umrah" ||
                                    tripType === "Haji"
                                      ? "Cari tujuan..."
                                      : "Cari negara atau destinasi..."
                                  }
                                  onChange={(event) =>
                                    setDestinationQuery(
                                      event.target.value,
                                    )
                                  }
                                />
                              </div>

                              <div className="segadeals-picker-section">
                                <span className="segadeals-picker-label">
                                  Destinasi Populer
                                </span>

                                <div className="segadeals-picker-chips">
                                  {filteredDestinations.length >
                                  0 ? (
                                    filteredDestinations.map(
                                      (item) => (
                                        <button
                                          key={item}
                                          type="button"
                                          className={
                                            destination === item
                                              ? "is-active"
                                              : ""
                                          }
                                          onClick={() =>
                                            chooseDestination(
                                              item,
                                            )
                                          }
                                        >
                                          {item}
                                        </button>
                                      ),
                                    )
                                  ) : (
                                    <div className="segadeals-picker-empty">
                                      Tujuan tidak ditemukan.
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="segadeals-builder-block">
                    <div className="segadeals-builder-label">
                      <span>Traveler</span>
                      <small>
                        {travelerTotal} traveler
                      </small>
                    </div>

                    <div className="segadeals-traveler-grid">
                      {(
                        [
                          [
                            "adult",
                            "Dewasa",
                            "12 tahun ke atas",
                          ],
                          [
                            "child",
                            "Anak",
                            "2–11 tahun",
                          ],
                          [
                            "infant",
                            "Bayi",
                            "Di bawah 2 tahun",
                          ],
                        ] as const
                      ).map(
                        ([key, label, description]) => (
                          <div
                            className="segadeals-traveler-item"
                            key={key}
                          >
                            <div>
                              <strong>{label}</strong>
                              <span>{description}</span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="segadeals-stepper">
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                aria-label={`Kurangi ${label}`}
                                onClick={() =>
                                  updateTraveler(
                                    key,
                                    -1,
                                  )
                                }
                                disabled={
                                  travelers[key] === 0 ||
                                  (key === "adult" &&
                                    travelers.adult ===
                                      1 &&
                                    travelers.child ===
                                      0 &&
                                    travelers.infant ===
                                      0)
                                }
                              >
                                <Minus size={15} />
                              </button>

                              <strong>
                                {travelers[key]}
                              </strong>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                aria-label={`Tambah ${label}`}
                                onClick={() =>
                                  updateTraveler(
                                    key,
                                    1,
                                  )
                                }
                              >
                                <Plus size={15} />
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ),
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="segadeals-builder-grid">
                    <div className="segadeals-builder-field">
                      <label htmlFor="segadeals-budget">
                        Budget
                      </label>

                      <div className="segadeals-control">
                        <CircleDollarSign size={18} />

                        <span className="segadeals-control-prefix">
                          {currency}
                        </span>

                        <input
                          id="segadeals-budget"

                          value={budget}
                          placeholder={
                            currency === "IDR"
                              ? "Contoh 30000000"
                              : "Masukkan budget"
                          }
                          onChange={(event) =>

                              event.target.value.replace(

                          }

                      </div>

                      {numericBudget > 0 && (
                        <small className="segadeals-field-helper">
                          {budgetDisplay}
                        </small>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="segadeals-builder-field">
                      <label htmlFor="segadeals-date">

                      </label>

                      <div className="segadeals-picker-anchor">

                          ]

                            <div className="segadeals-calendar-head">

                                Atur Tanggal
                              </strong>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="segadeals-picker-close"
                                aria-label="Tutup"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  setDateOpen(false)
                                }
                              >
                                ×
                              </button>
                            </div>

                            <div className="segadeals-calendar-navigation">
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                aria-label="Bulan sebelumnya"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  moveCalendar(-1)
                                }
                              >
                                <ChevronDown
                                  size={18}
                                  style={{
                                    transform:
                                      "rotate(90deg)",
                                  }}
                                />
                              </button>

                              </div>

                                  moveCalendar(1)
                                }
                              >
                                <ChevronDown

                                    transform:

                                  }}
                                />
                              </button>
                            </div>

                              {calendarMonths.map(

function renderCouponIcon(type: string) {
  switch (type) {
    case "umrah":
      return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path
            d="M5 7.5V6a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h7l5 5v8.5a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V16"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M14 4v5h5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M4 12h8"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M8 9.5l4 2.5-4 2.5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
        </svg>
      );

    case "tour":
      return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path
            d="M3 15.5h8l8.5-8.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 0-2.1-2.1L9 13.5V5.8a1.8 1.8 0 0 0-3.6 0v3.6L3.2 8.6a1.3 1.3 0 0 0-1.7 1.2v1.1L4 12.2l-1 1.1v2.2Z"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.7"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M14.5 17.5h5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.7"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        </svg>
      );

    case "cashback":
      return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path
            d="M4 7.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 5h11A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 20 7.5v9A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 17.5 19h-11A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 4 16.5z"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
          />
          <path
            d="M4 9h16"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M12 12a2.5 2.5 0 1 0 0 5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M11.5 13.5h1.2a1 1 0 0 1 0 2h-1.4a1 1 0 0 0 0 2h1.2"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.5"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        </svg>
      );

    case "attraction":
      return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path
            d="M6 7.5h12a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v1a2 2 0 0 0-2 2a2 2 0 0 0 2 2v1a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H6a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-1a2 2 0 0 0 2-2a2 2 0 0 0-2-2v-1a2 2 0 0 1 2-2Z"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <path
            d="M9.2 14.5 14.8 9.5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <circle cx="9" cy="10" r="1" fill="currentColor" />
          <circle cx="15" cy="14" r="1" fill="currentColor" />
        </svg>
      );

    default:
      return <span>%</span>;
  }
}



function localAssetExists(src: string) {
  const relative = src.replace(/^\/+/, "");

  return fs.existsSync(
    path.join(process.cwd(), "public", relative),
  );
}
export function BenefitSection() {
  const icons = [BadgeCheck, CreditCard, ShieldCheck, Headphones];
  const benefitMessages = [
    {
      title: "benefit.bestPrice",
      description: "benefit.bestPriceDescription",
    },
    {
      title: "benefit.securePayment",

    },
    {
      title: "benefit.trustedService",
      description: "benefit.trustedServiceDescription",
    },
    {
      title: "benefit.customerSupport",
      description: "benefit.customerSupportDescription",
    },
  ] as const;

  return (
    <section className="section compact-section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="benefit-grid">
          {benefitMessages.map((benefit, index) => {

            return (

                <div className="benefit-icon">
                  <Icon size={24} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3>
                    <MarketplaceText messageKey={benefit.title} />
                  </h3>
                  <p>
                    <MarketplaceText messageKey={benefit.description} />
                  </p>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

      localAssetExists(item.image),
    );

  return (

         * FALLBACK UI DATA.
         *

         * Data berasal realtime dari Travel Ads
         * yang sudah disetujui/aktif.

export function PackageSection() {
  const categoryIcon = (item: (typeof featuredPackages)[number]) => {
    if (item.title.toLowerCase().includes("haji")) return Map;
    if (item.title.toLowerCase().includes("halal")) return Globe2;
    return Building2;
  };

  return (
    <section className="section" id="paket">
      <div className="container">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow="Rekomendasi"
          title={<MarketplaceText messageKey="home.nextTripPackages" />}
          description={<MarketplaceText messageKey="home.nextTripDescription" />}
          action={<MarketplaceText messageKey="common.viewAll" />}
        />

        <div className="product-grid">
          {featuredPackages.map((item) => (
            <a
              href={`/paket/${item.id}`}
              className="product-card reference-package-card"
              key={item.id}
            >
              <div className="reference-package-visual">
                <img src={item.image} alt={item.title} className="product-image" />
                <div className="reference-package-visual-overlay" />
                <span className="reference-package-category">
                  {item.title.toLowerCase().includes("haji") ? (
                      <MarketplaceText messageKey="category.hajj" />
                    ) : item.title.toLowerCase().includes("halal") ? (
                      <MarketplaceText messageKey="category.halalTour" />
                    ) : (
                      <MarketplaceText messageKey="category.umrah" />
                    )}
                </span>
                <span
  className="reference-package-travel-logo"
  title={item.travelName || "Travel"}
>
  {item.travelLogo ? (
    <img
      src={item.travelLogo}
      alt={`Logo ${item.travelName || "Travel"}`}
      loading="lazy"
    />
  ) : null}
</span>
                <span className="reference-package-rating">
                  <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {item.rating} ({item.reviews})
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="product-body reference-package-body">
                <span className="product-location">{item.location}</span>
                <h3>{item.title}</h3>

                <div className="reference-package-meta">
                  <span>{item.duration ?? <MarketplaceText messageKey="package.durationAvailable" />}</span>
                  <span>· {item.location.split(" · ")[0]}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="product-price">
                  <small><MarketplaceText messageKey="common.price" /></small>
                  <strong><MarketplacePrice amountInIdr={item.price} /></strong>
                  <span className="reference-package-arrow"><ArrowRight size={15} /></span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </a>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function SectionHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  action,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  description?: ReactNode;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="section-heading">
      <div>
        {eyebrow && <span className="section-eyebrow">{eyebrow}</span>}
        <h2>{title}</h2>
        {description && <p>{description}</p>}
      </div>

      {action && (
        <a href="#">
          {action}
          <ChevronRight size={18} />
        </a>
      )}
    </div>
  
    
  );
}

import {

} from "lucide-react";

import {
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  useRouter,
} from "next/navigation";


type Props = {
  category: string;
  initialOrigin: string;
  initialDestination: string;
  initialTravelers: string;
  initialDate: string;
};


type Panel =
  | "origin"
  | "destination"
  | "traveler"
  | "date"
  | null;


const origins = [
  "Jakarta",
  "Makassar",
  "Surabaya",
  "Medan",
  "Bandung",
  "Yogyakarta",
  "Semarang",
  "Balikpapan",
];


const umrahTypes = [
  "Hemat",
  "Regular",
  "Plus",
  "First",
];


const categoryOptions: Record<string, string[]> = {
  Umrah: umrahTypes,

  Haji: [
    "Haji Reguler",
    "Haji Plus",
    "Haji Khusus",
  ],

  "Halal Tour": [
    "Jepang",
    "Korea Selatan",
    "Turkiye",
    "Dubai",
    "Eropa",
  ],

  "Tour Domestic": [
    "Bali",
    "Lombok",
    "Labuan Bajo",
    "Yogyakarta",
    "Raja Ampat",
  ],

  "Tour International": [
    "Jepang",
    "Korea Selatan",
    "Turkiye",
    "Dubai",
    "Singapura",
  ],

  SegaDeals: [
    "Umrah",
    "Halal Tour",
    "Tour Domestic",
    "Tour International",
  ],
};


function parseDate(
  value: string,
) {
  if (!value) {
    return "";
  }

  return value;
}


function formatDate(
  value: string,
) {
  if (!value) {
    return "Pilih tanggal";
  }

  const date =
    new Date(
      `${value}T00:00:00`,
    );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(
      date.getTime(),
    )
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
    "id-ID",
    {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric",
    },
  ).format(date);
}


export function SearchEditBar({
  category,
  initialOrigin,
  initialDestination,
  initialTravelers,
  initialDate,
}: Props) {

  const router =
    useRouter();


  const [panel, setPanel] =
    useState<Panel>(
      null,
    );


  const [origin, setOrigin] =
    useState(
      initialOrigin ||
      "Jakarta",
    );


  const initialUmrahType =
    umrahTypes.includes(
      initialDestination,
    )
      ? initialDestination
      : "Hemat";


  const [
    destination,
    setDestination,
  ] =
    useState(
      category === "Umrah"
        ? initialUmrahType
        : initialDestination,
    );


  const [
    travelers,
    setTravelers,
  ] =
    useState(
      Math.max(
        1,
        Number(
          initialTravelers ||
          2,
        ),
      ),
    );


  const [
    selectedDate,
    setSelectedDate,
  ] =
    useState(
      parseDate(
        initialDate,
      ),
    );


  const options =
    useMemo(
      () =>
        categoryOptions[
          category
        ] || [],
      [
        category,
      ],
    );


  const canSearch =
    origin.trim().length > 0 &&
    destination.trim().length > 0 &&
    travelers > 0 &&
    selectedDate.length > 0;


  function submitSearch() {

    if (!canSearch) {
      return;
    }

    const params =
      new URLSearchParams();

    params.set(
      "category",
      category,
    );

    params.set(
      "origin",
      origin,
    );

    params.set(
      "destination",
      destination,
    );

    params.set(
      "travelers",
      String(
        travelers,
      ),
    );

    params.set(
      "date",
      selectedDate,
    );

    setPanel(null);

    router.push(
      `/search?${params.toString()}`,
    );
  }


  return (
    <div className="search-edit-bar">


      <div className="search-edit-control">

        <button
          type="button"
          className="search-edit-field"
          onClick={() =>
            setPanel(
              panel === "origin"
                ? null
                : "origin",
            )
          }
        >
          <MapPin size={18} />

          <span>
            <small>Dari</small>

            <strong>
              {origin}
            </strong>
          </span>
        </button>


        {panel === "origin" && (

          <div className="search-edit-dropdown">

            <strong>
              Kota keberangkatan
            </strong>

            <div className="search-edit-option-list">

              {origins.map(
                (value) => (

                  <button
                    key={value}
                    type="button"
                    className={
                      value === origin
                        ? "active"
                        : ""
                    }
                    onClick={() => {
                      setOrigin(
                        value,
                      );

                      setPanel(
                        null,
                      );
                    }}
                  >
                    {value}
                  </button>

                ),
              )}

            </div>

          </div>

        )}

      </div>


      <div className="search-edit-control">

        <button
          type="button"
          className="search-edit-field"
          onClick={() =>
            setPanel(
              panel ===
                "destination"
                ? null
                : "destination",
            )
          }
        >

          <MapPin size={18} />

          <span>

            <small>
              {category === "Umrah"
                ? "Tujuan Umrah"
                : `Tujuan ${category}`}
            </small>

            <strong>
              {destination ||
                "Pilih tujuan"}
            </strong>

          </span>

        </button>


        {panel ===
          "destination" && (

          <div className="search-edit-dropdown search-edit-destination">

            <strong>
              {category === "Umrah"
                ? "Pilih Paket Umrah"
                : "Pilih Tujuan"}
            </strong>


            {category ===
            "Umrah" ? (

              <div className="search-umrah-options">

                {umrahTypes.map(
                  (value) => (

                    <button
                      key={value}
                      type="button"
                      className={
                        value ===
                        destination
                          ? "active"
                          : ""
                      }
                      onClick={() => {
                        setDestination(
                          value,
                        );

                        setPanel(
                          null,
                        );
                      }}
                    >
                      {value}
                    </button>

                  ),
                )}

              </div>

            ) : (

              <div className="search-edit-option-list">

                {options.map(
                  (value) => (

                    <button
                      key={value}
                      type="button"
                      className={
                        value ===
                        destination
                          ? "active"
                          : ""
                      }
                      onClick={() => {
                        setDestination(
                          value,
                        );

                        setPanel(
                          null,
                        );
                      }}
                    >
                      {value}
                    </button>

                  ),
                )}

              </div>

            )}

          </div>

        )}

      </div>


      <div className="search-edit-control">

        <button
          type="button"
          className="search-edit-field"
          onClick={() =>
            setPanel(
              panel === "date"
                ? null
                : "date",
            )
          }
        >

          <CalendarDays
            size={18}
          />

          <span>

            <small>
              Tanggal
            </small>

            <strong>
              {formatDate(
                selectedDate,
              )}
            </strong>

          </span>

        </button>


        {panel === "date" && (

          <div className="search-edit-dropdown search-edit-date">

            <strong>
              Pilih tanggal
            </strong>

            <input
              type="date"
              value={
                selectedDate
              }
              min={
                new Date()
                  .toISOString()
                  .slice(
                    0,
                    10,
                  )
              }
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSelectedDate(
                  event.target.value,
                );

                if (
                  event.target.value
                ) {
                  setPanel(
                    null,
                  );
                }
              }}
            />

          </div>

        )}

      </div>


      <div className="search-edit-control">

        <button
          type="button"
          className="search-edit-field"
          onClick={() =>
            setPanel(
              panel ===
                "traveler"
                ? null
                : "traveler",
            )
          }
        >

          <UsersRound
            size={18}
          />

          <span>

            <small>
              Traveler
            </small>

            <strong>
              {travelers} orang
            </strong>

          </span>

        </button>


        {panel ===
          "traveler" && (

          <div className="search-edit-dropdown search-edit-traveler">

            <div>

              <strong>
                Traveler
              </strong>

              <small>
                Jumlah peserta
              </small>

            </div>


            <div className="search-edit-counter">

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={
                  travelers <= 1
                }
                onClick={() =>
                  setTravelers(
                    (value) =>
                      Math.max(
                        1,
                        value - 1,
                      ),
                  )
                }
              >
                −
              </button>


              <strong>
                {travelers}
              </strong>


              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setTravelers(
                    (value) =>
                      value + 1,
                  )
                }
              >
                +
              </button>

            </div>

          </div>

        )}

      </div>


      <button
        type="button"
        className="search-edit-submit"
        disabled={!canSearch}
        onClick={
          submitSearch
        }
      >

        <Search size={18} />

        Cari

      </button>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import {
  SearchEditBar,
} from "@/components/search/SearchEditBar";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Building2,
  ChevronRight,
  Filter,
  Globe2,

  Star,

  X,

import {
  featuredPackages,
  type TravelProduct,
} from "@/data/home";

import {
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";


type Props = {
  category: string;
  origin: string;
  destination: string;
  travelers: string;
  date: string;
};


const supportedAirlines = [
  "Garuda Indonesia",
  "Saudia",
  "Emirates",
  "Qatar Airways",
  "Etihad Airways",

const umrahFilterOptions = [
  "Hemat",
  "Regular",
  "Plus",
  "First",
];

type SortMode =
  | "relevant"
  | "lowest"
  | "highest"
  | "departure";


type ViewMode =
  | "grid"
  | "list";


type PackageMeta = {
  airline: string;
  departure: string;
};



const sponsoredPackages = [

    image:

const packageMeta: Record<string, PackageMeta> = {
  "umrah-premium-12d": {
    airline: "Garuda Indonesia",
    departure: "2026-12-10",
  },

  "umrah-plus-turkiye": {
    airline: "Turkish Airlines",
    departure: "2026-12-15",

    airline: "Japan Airlines",
    departure: "2026-12-18",
